# H09 evidence / Протокол H09

Review required; no automatic HW VERIFIED promotion.

## Reviewed result

The run was intentionally interrupted at the first CV display gate after its
printed preflight was found to contain the obsolete CC40 range. The CV2 point
had stable measurements but was not operator-reviewed: PSU `MEAS:VOLT?=1.99481`,
`MEAS:CURR?=0.0498281`; KEL103 `MEAS:VOLT?=1.9883V`,
`MEAS:CURR?=0.0493A`, `MEAS:POW?=0.0980W`; DMM7510 `:READ?=1.987819`.
`SYST:ERR?` returned `0, "No error"`; after interruption PSU output readback was
OFF and KEL103 `:INP?` returned `OFF`. The operator disconnected the KEL103,
DMM7510 and Series jumper. Outcome: ABORTED by operator gate interruption;
this point is evidence only, not a pass. The preflight text was corrected and
the full offline suite passed 111/111 before the next run.
